## 付録B 略語集

| 略語 | 展開 | 和訳 |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **ACT** | activated clotting time | 活性化凝固時間 |
| **AF** | atrial fibrillation | 心房細動 |
| **AR / AI** | aortic regurgitation / insufficiency | 大動脈弁閉鎖不全 |
| **AR（工学）** | augmented reality | 拡張現実 |
| **ARE** | aortic root enlargement | 大動脈基部拡大 |
| **AS** | aortic stenosis | 大動脈弁狭窄 |
| **ASD** | atrial septal defect | 心房中隔欠損 |
| **AV groove** | atrioventricular groove | 房室溝 |
| **AVR** | aortic valve replacement | 大動脈弁置換 |
| **BITA** | bilateral internal thoracic arteries | 両側内胸動脈 |
| **BSA** | body surface area | 体表面積 |
| **CABG** | coronary artery bypass grafting | 冠動脈バイパス術 |
| **Com–Com** | commissure-to-commissure | 交連間距離 |
| **CPB** | cardiopulmonary bypass | 人工心肺 |
| **CTA** | computed tomography angiography | CT血管造影 |
| **CUSUM** | cumulative sum | 累積和（学習曲線の解析法） |
| **CRT** | cardiac resynchronization therapy | 心臓再同期療法 |
| **Cx / LCX** | circumflex coronary artery | 左回旋枝 |
| **ECG-CT** | ECG-gated cardiac CT | 心電図同期心臓CT |
| **EF** | ejection fraction | 駆出率 |
| **HCM / HOCM** | (obstructive) hypertrophic cardiomyopathy | （閉塞性）肥大型心筋症 |
| **HCR** | hybrid coronary revascularization | ハイブリッド冠血行再建 |
| **ICS** | intercostal space | 肋間 |
| **IPTW** | inverse probability of treatment weighting | 逆確率重み付け |
| **ITA / IMA** | internal thoracic / mammary artery | 内胸動脈 |
| **LAA / LAAC** | left atrial appendage (closure) | 左心耳（閉鎖） |
| **LAD** | left anterior descending artery | 左前下行枝 |
| **LITA / LIMA** | left internal thoracic / mammary artery | 左内胸動脈 |
| **LOM** | ligament of Marshall | Marshall靱帯 |
| **LVOT** | left ventricular outflow tract | 左室流出路 |
| **MAC** | mitral annular calcification | 僧帽弁輪石灰化 |
| **MACCE** | major adverse cardiac and cerebrovascular events | 主要心脳血管イベント |
| **mCAD** | minimum coronary annular distance | 冠動脈–弁輪最短距離 |
| **MDR** | Medical Device Regulation (EU 2017/745) | 欧州医療機器規則 |
| **mGEARS** | modified global evaluative assessment of robotic skills | ロボット手技の総合評価尺度（改変版） |
| **MICS** | minimally invasive cardiac surgery | 低侵襲心臓手術 |
| **MIDCAB** | minimally invasive direct coronary artery bypass | 小開胸直視下冠動脈バイパス |
| **MR（画像）** | mixed reality | 複合現実 |
| **MR（弁）** | mitral regurgitation | 僧帽弁閉鎖不全 |
| **M-TEER** | mitral transcatheter edge-to-edge repair | 経カテーテル僧帽弁接合部形成 |
| **MVr / MVR** | mitral valve repair / replacement | 僧帽弁形成／置換 |
| **NLC** | number of procedures to overcome the learning curve | 学習曲線克服に要する症例数 |
| **O/E** | observed to expected ratio | 実測／予測比 |
| **OFCCJ** | osteophyte of the first costochondral joint | 第1肋軟骨結合の骨棘 |
| **OM** | obtuse marginal branch | 鈍縁枝 |
| **OPCAB** | off-pump coronary artery bypass | 心拍動下冠動脈バイパス |
| **PAL** | posterior annular length | 後方弁輪長 |
| **PCI** | percutaneous coronary intervention | 経皮的冠動脈形成術 |
| **PDA / PLB** | posterior descending artery / posterolateral branch | 後下行枝／後側壁枝 |
| **PI** | pulsatility index | 拍動係数（グラフト流量評価） |
| **PPM** | patient–prosthesis mismatch | 患者–人工弁不適合 |
| **PVL** | paravalvular leak | 弁周囲逆流 |
| **RA-MIDCAB** | robotic-assisted MIDCAB | ロボット支援MIDCAB |
| **RAVR** | robotic aortic valve replacement | ロボット大動脈弁置換 |
| **RITA / RIMA** | right internal thoracic / mammary artery | 右内胸動脈 |
| **SAM** | systolic anterior motion | 僧帽弁前尖の収縮期前方運動 |
| **SAVR** | surgical aortic valve replacement | 外科的大動脈弁置換 |
| **SLDA** | single-leaflet device attachment | 片尖のみへのデバイス付着 |
| **SP** | single-port (da Vinci SP) | 単孔式 |
| **STS** | The Society of Thoracic Surgeons | 米国胸部外科学会 |
| **SVC / IVC** | superior / inferior vena cava | 上／下大静脈 |
| **TAVR** | transcatheter aortic valve replacement | 経カテーテル大動脈弁置換 |
| **TBS** | time-based score | 時間ベーススコア |
| **TEAVR** | totally endoscopic aortic valve replacement | 完全内視鏡下大動脈弁置換 |
| **TECAB** | totally endoscopic coronary artery bypass | 完全内視鏡下冠動脈バイパス |
| **TEE / TTE** | transesophageal / transthoracic echocardiography | 経食道／経胸壁心エコー |
| **TEER** | transcatheter edge-to-edge repair | 経カテーテル接合部形成 |
| **TR / TVr** | tricuspid regurgitation / valve repair | 三尖弁閉鎖不全／形成 |
| **TTF** | transit-time flow (measurement) | 通過時間流量測定 |
| **UPE** | unilateral pulmonary edema | 片側肺水腫 |
| **VRA** | vertical right atriotomy | 垂直右房切開 |
